**Rolling window:** 2026-09-14 through 2026-09-20

## Best date plans

### 2026-09-19 — Newlyweeds outdoor screening + Agi's Counter

**Newlyweeds outdoor screening, then a meal.** Eastern European cafe at Agi's Counter. It is a 17-minute walk from the venue. No published start time, so check before you set the table.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=Brooklyn+Ave+%26+Prospect+Pl%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11213&destination=818+Franklin+Avenue%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11225&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://rooftopfilms.com/event/newlyweeds/) · [Restaurant](https://www.agiscounter.com/)

### 2026-09-14 — Runner Up + Pablo Masis and Jim Seeley Trio

**Dinner, then Pablo Masis and Jim Seeley Trio.** American wine bar at Runner Up. It is a 16-minute walk from the venue. Pablo Masis and Jim Seeley Trio starts at 7 pm, so book the table early.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=367+7th+Avenue%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11215&destination=58+7th+Ave%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11217&travelmode=walking) · [Event](http://bqcm.org) · [Event booking](https://www.songkick.com/concerts/43332701-pablo-masis-at-brooklyn-conservatory-of-music) · [Restaurant](https://www.runnerupbk.com/)

### 2026-09-14 — Mixed-Media Collage at the Art Cart + Yellow Rose

**Mixed-Media Collage at the Art Cart, then a meal.** Tex-Mex at Yellow Rose. It is 1.5 miles from the venue, so budget a subway stop between the two. It starts at 2:30 pm, which leaves the evening for the table.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=Bryant+Park%2C+New+York%2C+NY+10018&destination=102+3rd+Avenue%2C+New+York%2C+NY+10003&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://bryantpark.org/calendar/event/art-cart-workshop-230pm/2026-09-14) · [Restaurant](https://www.yellowrosenyc.com/)

### 2026-09-19 — MoMA PS1 Open House + R40

**MoMA PS1 Open House, then a meal.** Argentine at R40. It is a 6-minute walk from the venue. It starts at 10:30 am, which leaves the evening for the table.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=22-25+Jackson+Ave%2C+Long+Island+City%2C+NY+11101&destination=47-16+Vernon+Boulevard%2C+Long+Island+City%2C+NY+11101&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://www.momaps1.org/en/events/839-open-house) · [Event booking](https://www.eventbrite.com/e/open-house-tickets-1997780186316) · [Restaurant](https://www.r40lic.com/)

### 2026-09-14 — Claro + Franklin Park Reading Series fall kickoff

**Dinner, then Franklin Park Reading Series fall kickoff.** Oaxacan at Claro. It is 1.6 miles from the venue, so budget a subway stop between the two. Franklin Park Reading Series fall kickoff starts at 8 pm, so book the table early.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=284+3rd+Avenue%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11215&destination=618+St+Johns+Pl%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11238&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://www.facebook.com/FranklinParkReadingSeries) · [Restaurant](https://www.clarobk.com/)

### 2026-09-14 — Mary's Fish Camp + Frances Ha Outdoor Screening

**Dinner, then Frances Ha Outdoor Screening.** Seafood at Mary's Fish Camp. It is a 7-minute walk from the venue. Frances Ha Outdoor Screening starts at 8 pm, so book the table early.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=64+Charles+Street%2C+New+York%2C+NY+10014&destination=Gansevoort+Plaza%2C+New+York%2C+NY+10014&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://rooftopfilms.com/event/frances-ha/) · [Restaurant](https://www.marysfishcamp.com/)

### 2026-09-14 — Runner Up + The Moth StorySLAM: Nine to Five

**Dinner, then The Moth StorySLAM: Nine to Five.** American wine bar at Runner Up. It is a 15-minute walk from the venue. The Moth StorySLAM: Nine to Five starts at 8 pm, so book the table early.

### 2026-09-14 — Claro + Book Launch: Saying No to New by Eric Athas

**Dinner, then Book Launch: Saying No to New by Eric Athas.** Oaxacan at Claro. It is 1.8 miles from the venue, so budget a subway stop between the two. Book Launch: Saying No to New by Eric Athas starts at 7 pm, so book the table early.

Open-air night market behind the New York Hall of Science with about a hundred independent food, art, and merchandise vendors, deliberately kept low-priced.

## Where to eat

Use the corrected set below: keep the strongest dated menus, active specials, real September/August openings, and bounded market dates; reject duplicate, closed, or superseded cards.

Dining research: 2026-09-14T10:40:21+00:00

No Michelin-star claim is retained for any NYC candidate. Kirbee's source only says the Texas collaborators' restaurants have Michelin nods/Young Chef context, not that Kirbee's has a star or Michelin selection.
